NewUNIT-0033/0034 are bounded source-grounded planned formations only. No newIDENTITY/CHAR/READY or unit-synthesis maturity. Older revisions,includingREV-0044,remain current. All25 individual analytical routes are existing local identities, including canonical Coney/rokusaki-connie-presented, 上杉・ウエバス・キョーコ and usuta-sumire. Reported Matsuri, Saori, grandfather and Coney retain reporting frames; no present own turn is manufactured. Historical identity spellings and current published revisions, including REV-0044's announcement-versus-departure correction, remain unchanged. Two new UNIT_STATE IDs describe an explicitly Christmas-limited Haru/Kajika formation and the named planned Team Illusion act. They add no new person, formal contract, permanent membership or unit-synthesis promotion.
